- **Default to the earliest level that covers the scene.** A level-1 scene uses only the level-0 and level-1 facts of the compendium.
- **Reveal in order.** When a character first learns a secret, use the line above as the earliest point; do not let an earlier-book character speak with the knowledge.
- **Keep levels in clearly marked sections** ("Later developments") when writing new documents.
- **Check a character's knowledge, not the reader's.** A narrator may know a level-3 fact while a level-1 character still believes the churches destroyed the Order.

---

## Tone and Voice Notes

### 1. Summary

The register the books use, how forms of address work, and a condensed voice note for the main speakers, each tied to a line.

### 2. Facts

**The series' register**
- **Deadpan humour inside the setting**: Jason answers an unreadable item description with "Question marks. That's enlightening. Do I have to pay a wizard to identify items?" (Book 1, Ch1 L108).
- **Modern references in a fantasy world**: he suggests the Builder and Knowledge "play Mario Kart" (Book 3, Ch57 L87) and likens a projected face to Emperor Palpatine (Book 9, Ch6 L17).
- **Humour does not cancel stakes**: Jason jokes about kneeling to the Storm King, and then answers a threat of execution flatly: "Die on my feet. I've done it before" (Book 7, Ch59 L81-83).

**Forms of address**
- Titles for officials: "Instructor Trenslow" and "Instructor Spalding" during an assessment (Book 1, Ch54 L36); "Priestess Pellin" (Book 7, Ch9 L18).
- Messengers are addressed as lord whatever their sex (Book 9, Ch56 L13), and a Voice of the Will as "Voice Kaal" (Book 9, Ch56 L14).

**Condensed voice notes**
- **Jason**: sarcastic, deferential only when rank forces it, with a dry understatement when he is grieving ("It's not a celebratory time", Book 5, Ch20 L25).
- **Rufus**: courteous and formal, apologising for discourtesy to a priestess (Book 7, Ch9 L18), gesturing with a sausage on a stick while explaining guild quotas (Book 7, Ch12 L22-23).
- **Farrah**: lectures on any subject (Book 1, Ch23 L15-17) and is blunt when angry ("Barbou belongs to me", Book 6, Ch26 L9).
- **Sophie**: wary of kindness (Book 2, Ch1 L77-80), silent about pain (Book 2, Ch45 L69-72).
- **Neil**: tentative before he commits (Book 2, Ch27 L46).
- **Belinda**: delighted by her own power (Book 2, Ch32 L72) and teasing (Book 7, Ch6 L42).
- **Emir**: smooth and showy, fond of grand entrances (Book 3, Ch5 L9) and a polished aside (Book 3, Ch69 L509).
- **Dawn**: composed and dry, lying lightly to Jason's face ("Of course I wasn't", Book 5, Ch20 L24).
- **Zara**: courtly with a threat in the smile (Book 2, Ch69 L17-19).
- **Mr North**: amused and unhurried (Book 5, Ch41 L33).
- **Raythe**: terse and final (Book 9, Ch97 L80). **Knowledge**: "detached and untouchable" (Book 1, Ch46 L16).

### 3. Using this in a story

- **Match the voice to the speaker's rank and culture**: Pallimustus officials use titles; Earth characters use first names and slang.
- **Do not invent catchphrases**; use the quoted cues and the character file's own voice section.
- **Keep jokes short and in-world**, and let the plot beat arrive after them rather than undercut them.

---

## NPC Cards: Characters First Appearing in Books 1-3

### 1. How to use these cards

One card per character, in alphabetical order. Each gives the role, appearance, a voice cue and the character's secret or spoiler note, with citations copied from the character file. For the full picture open the linked file. A card whose spoiler level is higher than this file's is wrapped in a level tag.

### 2. Cards

#### Anisa Lasalle

Valid from Book 1 to Book 3. Spoiler level 1. Character file: Anisa Lasalle.

- **Role**: Anisa Lasalle is a zealous elf priestess of Purity from the Lasalle family who guides Rufus's party in Book 1, is thrown out for killing a captive, and dies serving the Builder's cult alliance in Book 3. Cold, righteous and quick to anger, she treats mercy as weakness.
- **Appearance**: An elf, platinum-haired and fair-skinned, in fitted white adventuring gear (Book 2, Ch10 L92; Book 3, Ch41 L53).
- **Voice**: Cold, righteous and quick to anger.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Jason notes most of the people he first met in this world are dead (Book 3, Ch60 L5).

#### Beth Cavendish

Valid from Book 1 to Book 3. Spoiler level 1. Character file: Beth Cavendish.

- **Role**: Beth Cavendish is a bronze-rank Greenstone adventurer and water healer who leads a team in the Ustei campaign and the City of Fallen Echoes. Competent and brisk, she gives orders without ceremony.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the lines read.
- **Voice**: Competent and brisk; a clear team leader who gives orders without ceremony.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Callum Morse

- **Role**: A gold-rank stealth assassin, former teammate of Emir and of Rufus's parents, whose long hunt for Sophie's mother Melody Jain drives his later scenes.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the character file.
- **Voice**: Quiet, patient and unfazed; he lets a hard decision stand and speaks plainly. "Sensible," Callum agrees (Book 2, Ch36 L79).
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing recorded.

#### Cheryl Asano

Valid from Book 1 to Book 5. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Cheryl Asano.

- **Role**: Cheryl Asano is Jason's mother, from a large Australian farming and mining family. She is the one in the family fascinated with Japan, a strong-willed realtor who shows up in Book 4 stunned at her dead son's return.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the lines read.
- **Voice**: Strong-willed and protective; her grief at Jason's disappearance colours her early scenes. Cue: the dissatisfied look she can't hide.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Clarissa Ventress

Valid from Book 1 to Book 3. Spoiler level 1. Character file: Clarissa Ventress.

- **Role**: Clarissa Ventress is one of the crime lords of Old City and holds Sophie's indenture. She uses Sophie to provoke the rash Cole Silva and learns what Lucian Lamprey wants in order to control him.
- **Appearance**: Dark cascading hair and a walk "so sultry she was almost swerving"; she looks only a few years older than the two women with her (Book 1, Ch43 L29).
- **Voice**: Cool, amused and calculating. Cue: "dear Sophie."
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Cole Silva

Valid from Book 1 to Book 9. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Cole Silva.

- **Role**: Cole Silva is the newest of Old City's Big Three crime lords, who inherits his father's operation and, with Lucian Lamprey, orchestrates Jason's kidnapping and star seed implantation. Arrested in Book 3, his organisation falls into chaos.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the lines read.
- **Voice**: Predatory and entitled. Cue: the rash move his father always prevented.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Cressida Vane

Valid from Book 1 to Book 2. Spoiler level 1. Character file: Cressida Vane.

- **Role**: Cressida Vane is the matriarch of the blood-cult family that holds Jason and his friends in Book 1. Beautiful, haughty and weary of her family's ambitions, she insists they are "a nice, traditional blood cult family" and she is killed in the confrontation.
- **Appearance**: A young-looking woman in her early thirties with olive skin and dark hair, "no older than her son Landemere" (Book 1, Ch11 L65).
- **Voice**: Haughty, weary of her family's ambitions. Cue: "A nice, traditional blood cult family."
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Her body ends up on the stone floor at the edge of the chamber rather than in the pool (Book 1, Ch13 L55); Jason later alludes to her head being "smashed open" (Book 1, Ch97 L122).

#### Dustin Kettering

- **Role**: An ice-armoured defender from the Kettering family who starts as one of Thadwick Mercer's offsiders, joins Rick's team in Book 2 and later fights beside Neil in Cardinas.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the character file.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing recorded.

#### Emir Bahadir

- **Role**: Emir Bahadir is a gold-rank treasure hunter, patron of the Reaper contest and owner of a cloud ship and palace that becomes Jason's team's base.
- **Appearance**: Dark-skinned, with dark curly hair tied back behind his head, "midnight skin" and sleek clothes (Book 2, Ch31 L90; Book 1, Ch101 L64).
- **Voice**: Urbane, amused and generous, with a showman's love of timing; he turns serious quickly when someone is in danger. Cue: "I'm hosting a grand event.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: He knew of the Order of the Reaper's return long before the team (Book 9, Ch27 L65).

#### Hester Maharala

Valid from Book 1 to Book 6. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Hester Maharala.

- **Role**: Hester Maharala is Emir's portal user and head of logistics, born in Jayapura in Pranay. Warm, travelled and practical, she gets people where they need to be and keeps Emir's operations running.
- **Appearance**: The Asiatic-looking person Jason sees first in this world, closer to South Asian than his own Japanese features (Book 2, Ch34 L55).
- **Voice**: Warm, travelled and practical.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Hudson Kettering

Valid from Book 1 to Book 2. Spoiler level 1. Character file: Hudson Kettering.

- **Role**: Hudson Kettering is the huge earth-essence front-liner of Beth Cavendish's team and cousin of Dustin Kettering. He is a stone-shielding bruiser who meets Jason on the sand barge assault, fights Humphrey in a Geller-versus-Cavendish contest, and joins the Reaper trial expedition.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the character file.
- **Voice**: Large, quiet and steady: he lets Beth talk, gives his team a wall to stand behind and says little. He is protective of his cousin.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing recorded.

#### Kaito Asano

- **Role**: Kaito Asano is Jason's older brother, a year his senior, married to Amy, with two daughters, Hana and Jace. Easy-going and socially skilled, he is the "well-received original" Jason grew up in the shadow of.
- **Appearance**: Jason's face is more angular than his brother's; Kaito has a "signature easy-going smile" that Jason spent years learning to copy (Book 4, Ch25 L44).
- **Voice**: Warm, teasing, effortless and brave without announcing it. Cue: "G'day...
- **Secret or spoiler note**: His death is why Amy's children grow up without a father "because he followed me" (Book 7, Ch1 L15).

#### Landemere Vane

Valid from Book 1 to Book 7. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Landemere Vane.

- **Role**: Landemere Vane is the Vane heir whose failed summoning pulls Jason to Pallimustus. He is the first person Jason meets and the first he kills, which leaves Jason shaken for weeks.
- **Appearance**: His mother looks to be in her early thirties "no older than her son Landemere" (Book 1, Ch11 L65); no other description appears in the lines read.
- **Voice**: Not heard on the page; remembered as ambitious and out of his depth.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Clive says random dimensional events connecting worlds are rare but more frequent because of the link between the worlds (Book 7, Ch55 L64).

#### Lucian Lamprey

Valid from Book 1 to Book 9. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Lucian Lamprey.

- **Role**: Lucian Lamprey is the corrupt director of Greenstone's Magic Society branch, an elf with a fighter's physique "banished to Greenstone for previous improprieties". He treats the Fortress as his office, orchestrates Jason's kidnapping with Cole Silva, and is cut down by Danielle Geller and imprisoned.
- **Appearance**: An uncommonly muscular elf who "would not look out of place in the fighting pits himself", in expensive clothes (Book 1, Ch42 L26).
- **Voice**: Smug, indulgent and quick to blame. Cue: the contented sigh.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Nicolas Hendren

Valid from Book 2 to Book 3. Spoiler level 1. Character file: Nicolas Hendren.

- **Role**: Nicolas Hendren is the archbishop of the Church of Purity, a silver-rank barrier-and-force specialist who flees the inquiry and joins the Builder cult's camp before Jason's team kills him. Stiff, proud and rule-bound, he wants Purity's dignity even as it is dragged into the cult's plans.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the lines read.
- **Voice**: Stiff, proud and rule-bound.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: He took a large part of Purity's funds with him (Book 3, Ch60 L12).

#### Padma Parsell

Valid from Book 2 to Book 3. Spoiler level 1. Character file: Padma Parsell.

- **Role**: Padma Parsell is an adventurer from Remore Academy and Farrah's half-disciple, who leads a Vitesse team in the City of Fallen Echoes. Keen, grieving and quick to speak her mind, she treats Jason as a rival after Farrah's death.
- **Appearance**: Onyx skin and fiery eyes typical of her people; unaffected by heat (Book 2, Ch39 L13).
- **Voice**: Keen, grieving and quick to speak her mind.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Timos

Valid from Book 2 to Book 3. Spoiler level 1. Character file: Timos.

- **Role**: Timos is a Builder cultist embedded in Greenstone for years who regrets going along with Thadwick's self-recruitment, kills Frank, and stands with Zato in the Vane estate ritual. He is careful, patient and horrified by amateurs.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the lines read; he wears dismissive sneers until Thadwick wipes one off (Book 2, Ch41 L89).
- **Voice**: Careful, patient and horrified by amateurs. Cue: a dismissive sneer.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Valdis Volaire

- **Role**: A swordmaster prince of the Kingdom of Mirrors whom Clive meets in the City of Fallen Echoes; he is cheerful, reckless and a rival and friend to Jason's team.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the character file.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing recorded.

#### Zato

Valid from Book 3 to Book 3. Spoiler level 1. Character file: Zato.

- **Role**: Zato is the silver-rank leader of the Builder cult's local forces in Book 3, a metal-bodied shapeshifter who recruits Thadwick, drives the cult's survivors to the Vane Estate and is the final opponent of Jason's team. He is competent, unfazed and sure of victory.
- **Appearance**: A silvery metal body that flows "like quicksilver", reshaping itself to produce combat abilities (Book 3, Ch67 L69).
- **Voice**: Measured, zealous and confident until the very end. Cue: "I see your true potential."
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

## NPC Cards: Characters First Appearing in Books 4-6

### 1. How to use these cards

One card per character, in alphabetical order. Each gives the role, appearance, a voice cue and the character's secret or spoiler note, with citations copied from the character file. For the full picture open the linked file. A card whose spoiler level is higher than this file's is wrapped in a level tag.

### 2. Cards

#### Adrien Barbou

- **Role**: The Network's Lyon Operations Director, who imprisons Farrah in the dimensional fortress, rises to be the second "Mr East" and later follows Gerling.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the character file.
- **Voice**: Calculating and self-protective; he stays at the edge of a fight and tries to look insignificant (Book 6, Ch42 L22).
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing recorded.

#### Asya Karadeniz

Valid from Book 4 to Book 5. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Asya Karadeniz.

- **Role**: Asya Karadeniz is a school friend of Jason's from the Mid North Coast who works in Network magitech administration. She joins Jason's side in Book 4, crews Kaito's helicopter with Greg, enters Jason's spirit vault as their relationship deepens, and dies in the Berlin attack in Book 5.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the lines read.
- **Voice**: Brisk, wry and unafraid of Jason's reputation; modest about her desk job. Cue: "I'm just an administrator there."

#### Greg

- **Role**: Greg is an old school friend of Jason's who joins the Network defectors on Kaito's helicopter crew and is killed in Book 5. His family is taken into Asano Village afterward.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the lines read; he drives a 2017 Ford Taurus (Book 4, Ch66 L59).
- **Voice**: Nerdy, self-deprecating and braver than he believed. Cue: "never much more than an adequate lawyer."
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Jason's talk with his spectre follows his death (Book 5, Ch58 L3-14).

#### Ketevan Arziani

- **Role**: Ketevan Arziani is an analytical Network officer in Sydney, Anna Tilden's assistant, who later becomes Yumi Asano's chief of staff. Brisk and sceptical, she tests leaps of logic.
- **Appearance**: Jason guesses she is about thirty, athletic, with short brown hair (Book 4, Ch46 L82).
- **Voice**: Brisk and sceptical. Cue: testing leaps of logic.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

---

## NPC Cards: Characters First Appearing in Books 7-9

### 1. How to use these cards

One card per character, in alphabetical order. Each gives the role, appearance, a voice cue and the character's secret or spoiler note, with citations copied from the character file. For the full picture open the linked file. A card whose spoiler level is higher than this file's is wrapped in a level tag.

### 2. Cards

#### Amos Pensinata

- **Role**: A gold-rank adventurer with a soul-damaged aura who teaches Jason aura and soul techniques and is lost to an orb in Cardinas.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the character file.
- **Voice**: Calm and wry, with a teacher's patience and a soldier's scars.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing recorded.

#### Argy

Valid from Book 7 to Book 8. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Argy.

- **Role**: Argy (Emmett Dillivan) is a fruit grower in Palisaros who takes Jason, Rufus and Farrah into town and to the mayor, Pelli. Easygoing and proud of his town, he treats visitors as guests.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the lines read.
- **Voice**: Easygoing and proud of his town. Cue: "folks just call me Argy."
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Benella

Valid from Book 9 to Book 9. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Benella.

- **Role**: Benella is a servant of the messengers who works as Zolit's aide, brings Jason to the messengers' attention and is given one chance to prove herself valuable. Careful, watchful and afraid, she hides what she thinks behind a servant's neutrality.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the lines read; she carries a duffel of fight outfits (Book 9, Ch46 L44).
- **Voice**: Careful, watchful and afraid; she hides her thoughts behind servant neutrality. Cue: correcting a mispronounced name.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Fal Vin Garath

Valid from Book 9 to Book 9. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Fal Vin Garath.

- **Role**: Fal Vin Garath is a silver-rank messenger master who is abusive but within the bounds his peers accept. He detests acting at the behest of Jes Fin Kaal and launches a soul attack on Jason in Book 9.
- **Appearance**: Fair skin and golden hair, a physical counterpart to the other silver messenger (Book 9, Ch56 L5-7).
- **Voice**: Imperious and quick to anger; he assumes servants exist to answer him. Cue: "like a command from the heavens."
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Jeni Kavaloa

Valid from Book 7 to Book 8. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Jeni Kavaloa.

- **Role**: Jeni Kavaloa is the gold-rank leader of the mixed-force surge expedition in Book 7. She is irritated by the roster, suspects Vesper Rimaros of assembling it, and enlists Jason's astral magic to seal the astral aperture.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the lines read; she checks a pocket watch (Book 7, Ch47 L33).
- **Voice**: Brisk, suspicious and practical; she plans for the worst of a bad roster. Cue: checking her pocket watch.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Liara Rimaros

- **Role**: Liara Rimaros is a gold-rank Adventure Society special-operations officer and a lesser member of the Rimaros royal house.
- **Appearance**: Tall (her eldest daughter has "Liara's height") and gold-rank fast, flitting through streets unnoticed (Book 7, Ch17 L20; Book 8, Ch62 L39).
- **Voice**: Brisk, official and not unkind; she says what she needs and trusts competence. Cue: "Don't even think about it."
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Pelli

Valid from Book 7 to Book 8. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Pelli.

- **Role**: Pelli is a sharp-tongued core-using gold-ranker who keeps the town of Palisaros quiet and safe, is "Aunt Pelli" to the royal family, and is a contact for Jason in the Storm Kingdom. She fights a pirate captain outside Kasilaro and organises civilians at the mining facility.
- **Appearance**: Walks up the path from town with two celestines with emerald hair and eyes (Book 7, Ch18 L28).
- **Voice**: Blunt and watchful; protects the town's quiet life from outsiders. Cue: "The name's Pelli and I'll thank you to use it."
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

#### Sendira

Valid from Book 7 to Book 8. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Sendira.

- **Role**: Sendira is Melody Jain's second in command in the Order of Redeeming Light's local enclave: a silver-rank zealot and the person Melody trusts most.
- **Appearance**: Not described in the character file.
- **Voice**: Rigid, loyal and humourless: she speaks in doctrine, obeys Melody absolutely and treats questions as faults. When her plan fails she falls quiet rather than doubting.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing recorded.

#### Trenchant Moore

Valid from Book 7 to Book 9. Spoiler level 2. Character file: Trenchant Moore.

- **Role**: Trenchant Moore is a gold-rank human sword specialist of the Sapphire Crown royal guard who escorts Jason's group in Rimaros. Watchful and reserved, he reads places and people for danger.
- **Appearance**: A human in a guild of mostly celestines: pale-skinned and dark-haired, lean and angular with icy blue eyes (Book 7, Ch21 L13-14).
- **Voice**: Watchful and reserved; reads places and people for danger. Cue: dropping to one knee.
- **Secret or spoiler note**: Nothing hidden in the lines read.

## Quick Reference: Calendar and Festivals

### 1. The year

- **Months**: "twelve months of thirty days, divided up into early, mid and late stages of each season" (Book 1, Ch22 L19).
- **Extra days**: "five additional days that didn't count as days of the month for the solstices, the equinoxes and the new year, which was at the beginning of spring" (Book 1, Ch22 L19).
- **The week**: Jason acknowledges "you have a six-day week" (Book 6, Ch10 L41).
- **Day names**: "super-simple"; Jason muddles "Fifthday" and "Fourthday" (Book 8, Ch58 L19).
- **Field testing** is held at the start of every month (Book 1, Ch38 L158).
- **Surges** come about every ten years (Book 1, Ch31 L71).

### 2. Festivals the books name

| Festival | Detail | Line |
|---|---|---|
| Greenstone flower wine festival | Gary and friends attend; Jason later asks "How was the wine festival?" | Book 1, Ch49 L79, L113; Book 1, Ch99 L39 |
| A small-town festival | A lake town celebrated "for the better part of three weeks" as adventurers passed through | Book 2, Ch62 L3 |
| Rimaros post-surge festivals | "The post-surge festivals here are world-famous"; a week of celebration at the surge's end | Book 8, Ch37 L63; Book 9, Ch2 L16-19 |

### 3. Not in the text

The month and season names, a full list of day names and any religious holiday are not given in the lines read. The "holiday" Jason mentions in Book 2 is an Earth one (Book 2, Ch52 L32).

---

## Quick Reference: Coin Table

### 1. Conversion

"The basic coin of the realm was the lesser spirit coin. Iron spirit coins were worth a hundred lesser coins ... After that, it was ten iron to the bronze, ten bronze to the silver and ten silver to the gold" (Book 1, Ch61 L65).

| Coin | In lesser coins | Line |
|---|---|---|
| Lesser | 1 | Book 1, Ch61 L65 |
| Iron | 100 | Book 1, Ch61 L65 |
| Bronze | 1,000 (ten iron) | Book 1, Ch61 L65 |
| Silver | 10,000 (ten bronze) | Book 1, Ch61 L65 |
| Gold | 100,000 (ten silver); three gold coins are worth three hundred thousand units | Book 1, Ch61 L65 |

### 2. What each is for

- **Lesser coins** run almost all home and business magic devices such as lamps and showers; higher ranks would burn them out (Book 1, Ch80 L40-41).
- **Iron coins** are used by bulk traders, adventurers and the wealthy elite (Book 1, Ch61 L65).
- **Sustenance**: a bronze coin will sustain bronze-rank adventurers without stressing their bodies (Book 1, Ch15 L92).
- **Larger infrastructure**, such as the loop line, uses more powerful coins (Book 1, Ch80 L41).
- **Prices**: armour for an iron-rank adventurer is priced in bronze coins, which is unusual (Book 1, Ch61 L114).

### 3. Where coins come from

A farm that makes a thousand lesser coins would make only ten iron coins or one bronze coin (Book 1, Ch80 L39). The Magic Society manages production (Book 1, Ch80 L23).

---

## Quick Reference: Ranks and Lifespans

### 1. The ladder

The sequence is iron, bronze, silver, gold, diamond (Book 1, Ch22 L58). Normal people have fewer than four essences; four essences make iron rank (Book 1, Ch15 L100; Book 1, Ch21 L65-66). Each rank "has a suppressive effect on lower ranks" (Book 1, Ch19 L42).

### 2. Table

| Rank | Lifespan and body | Line |
|---|---|---|
| Normal | Fewer than four essences, no rank | Book 1, Ch15 L100 |
| Iron | Ageing is not noticeably slower; the body is purged of impurities and digestion changes | Book 1, Ch22 L58; Book 1, Ch22 L9; Book 2, Ch34 L73 |
| Bronze | Can live well past a hundred years | Book 1, Ch22 L58 |
| Silver | Can double that; a young riser looks young for decades; auras let the body levitate | Book 1, Ch22 L58; Book 4, Ch73 L92 |
| Gold | Lives for centuries and no longer needs to breathe | Book 1, Ch22 L58; Book 2, Ch34 L73 |
| Diamond | Rufus is not sure diamond rankers can die of old age; they can travel between physical realities | Book 1, Ch22 L58; Book 2, Ch25 L49 |

### 3. Other quick facts

- **Diamond is the highest rank** Rufus names (Book 1, Ch22 L50-51).
- **Diamonds in cities**: the Adventure Society keeps two or three in the largest cities "in case a diamond rank monster shows up" (Book 1, Ch22 L55).
- **Badges**: adventurers "need new badges at each rank", because the aura changes and tracking stones stop working (Book 3, Ch32 L88).
- **Progress to bronze** counts essences whose abilities are complete (Book 3, Ch14 L98).

---

## Spoiler-Safe Summary: Level 0, Public Setting

### 1. Read this before a session

This page uses only facts visible in the world from the first chapters. It reveals no plot event and no secret. Read it to run a scene in the setting without learning where the story goes.

### 2. The world

- **A magic-soaked world**: people absorb essences and gain abilities; four essences make an iron-rank essence user (Book 1, Ch15 L100; Book 1, Ch21 L65-66).
- **The rank ladder**: iron, bronze, silver, gold, diamond (Book 1, Ch22 L58). Each rank suppresses lower ranks (Book 1, Ch19 L42).
- **Lifespan grows with rank**: bronze can pass a hundred years, silver can double that, and gold lasts centuries (Book 1, Ch22 L58).
- **Surges**: magic spikes and monsters pour out about every ten years (Book 1, Ch31 L71).
- **Animals**: heidels, horse-sized, scaled and two-headed, replace horses (Book 1, Ch17 L106).
- **Magic density varies by place**: Greenstone is very low, mid-iron (Book 3, Ch34 L60).
- **Money**: ranked people can live on spirit coins (Book 1, Ch15 L92); see ../../04_Peoples_and_Cultures/Economy_and_Money.md for denominations.

### 3. Safe starting points

- An adventurer's work is monsters, cores and the Adventure Society's ranks; see ../../03_Magic_and_Adventuring/00_Magic_Overview.md.
- A scene can run in any city with its guilds, shops and officials; ../NPC_Rosters_by_Era.md lists names by era (check each name's level).

### 4. Do not state

Any named secret, any death, the plans of gods or astral beings, or anything about other worlds. Move to level 1 only when the table has read Books 1-3.

---

## Spoiler-Safe Summary: Level 1, Books 1-3

### 1. Read this before a session

Everything here is known by the end of Book 3. It includes Farrah's death and Jason's death, so it is not for a table that has not read those books. It leaves out everything from Book 4 on.

### 2. Situation

- **Setting**: Greenstone, a low-magic delta city. Jason arrives in late November (Book 4, Ch3 L112), and Old City is where the money is made (Book 1, Ch35 L19, L59).
- **Rank**: iron, then bronze; Path of Shadows reaches bronze in Book 3 (Book 3, Ch29 L23).
- **Local threats**: a blood cult in the hedge-maze estate (Book 1, Ch9 L50); the Ustei raiding coin convoys (Book 1, Ch83 L4); Old City's "Big Three" (Book 1, Ch50 L92).

### 3. Events and secrets by the end of Book 3

- **Jason is an outworlder**, and his team and Danielle know (Book 1, Ch21 L82-87; Book 1, Ch48 L101-105).
- **Farrah dies** covering civilians (Book 1, Ch113 L41).
- **The Order of the Reaper**: Emir says the churches scoured it away (Book 2, Ch25 L73); Shade says it survives and the trials are a lure (Book 2, Ch44 L72-78). Treat Emir's account as belief.
- **Star seeds**: great astral beings send followers star seeds (Book 2, Ch21 L26-28).
- **Emir's contest** frees 419 time-displaced priests (Book 2, Ch63 L9).
- **Book 3 opens** under an ecumenical inquiry into the church of Purity (Book 3, Ch1 L105); the reaper-trial astral space has twelve towers (Book 3, Ch36 L36).
- **Jason dies** covering the team's escape (Book 3, Ch69 L900).

### 4. Do not state

The Builder's identity beyond the cult, anything on Earth, the System, an astral kingdom, or what Purity is. Beliefs and lies to use are in ../Secrets_and_Rumours.md sections 3 and 4.

---

## Spoiler-Safe Summary: Level 2, Books 4-9

### 1. Read this before a session

### 2. Earth (Books 4-6)

- **Jason wakes on Earth** in early June, a year and a half after leaving (Book 4, Ch3 L112).
- **The EOA has seized Perth and Melbourne** (Book 4, Ch9 L76).
- **The Network goes public** as the Global Defense Network once monster waves begin (Book 5, Ch17 L7); transformation zones begin with Kostroma (Book 5, Ch44 L48-49).
- **Silver rank** is reached in Book 5 (Book 5, Ch31 L1).
- **The link between worlds** has siphoned magic from Pallimustus for centuries (Book 4, Ch68 L59).

### 3. Rimaros (Books 7-8)

- **The surge** is "some five years overdue" (Book 7, Ch1 L56).
- **One hundred and nineteen outworlders** appear at once (Book 7, Ch36 L86); the One Day War follows (Book 7, Ch66 L27).
- **Jason's death certificate** reads "died four times. So far." (Book 7, Ch17 L104).
- **The Order of Redeeming Light** converts towns (Book 8, Ch9 L40), and the gods may already have sanctioned Purity (Book 8, Ch48 L39-41).
- **The Astral Throne and Gate appear** (Book 8, Ch49 L105).

### 4. Yaresh (Book 9)

- **Jason is an astral king**, as a healer realises (Book 9, Ch62 L30-34).
- **Messengers camp outside Yaresh** (Book 9, Ch42 L80).

### 5. Do not state

## Starter Pack: Era 1, Greenstone (Books 1-2)

### 1. Setting

- **Where and when**: the low-magic colony city of Greenstone and its delta, from late November on Jason's arrival (Book 4, Ch3 L112) through the Reaper contest of Book 2.
- **Scale**: iron rank; Jason's badge is iron with one star (Book 1, Ch62 L66-67). Field testing happens at the start of each month (Book 1, Ch38 L158).
- **The city**: Old City is where the money is made and the Island is where it goes (Book 1, Ch35 L19, L59); see ../../06_Places/Greenstone.md.
- **Turning points**: Farrah dies covering civilians (Book 1, Ch113 L41); Emir's contest and the City of Fallen Echoes free 419 time-displaced priests (Book 2, Ch63 L9).

### 2. Three starting situations

1. **The Ustei in the desert**: nomads from the north are raiding spirit coin convoys far from home (Book 1, Ch83 L4; Book 1, Ch89 L50). *Why did they come south?*
2. **An indenture sale**: criminals' indentures are sold or auctioned to recoup the cost of their crimes (Book 1, Ch92 L32). *Who buys one, and why?*
3. **A corrupt branch**: the Magic Society's local director was banished to Greenstone for improprieties and uses the Fortress as his office (Book 1, Ch42 L25-30). *Who protects him?*

### 3. Cast and voice cues

- **Jason**: deadpan about the absurd: "Question marks. That's enlightening" (Book 1, Ch1 L108).
- **Rufus**: courteous and formal; gestures with a sausage on a stick while explaining guild quotas (Book 7, Ch12 L22-23).
- **Farrah**: lectures on any subject (Book 1, Ch23 L15-17); alive only until Book 1's end.
- **Gary**: deep and growling (Book 1, Ch9 L121).
- **Humphrey**: precise and a little shy about romance (Book 1, Ch47 L88-89).
- **Clive**: a lecturer who jokes about wrestling an eel (Book 1, Ch68 L92-94).
- **Sophie**: wary of kindness (Book 2, Ch1 L77-80).
- **Danielle Geller**: blunt, quick on a weakness (Book 1, Ch47 L89).
- **Emir**: smooth and showy (Book 3, Ch5 L9).
- **Authority**: Elspeth Arella (Book 1, Ch65 L60), Vincent Trenslow (Book 1, Ch54 L36), Lucian Lamprey and Pochard Finn (Book 1, Ch42 L25-30). More in ../NPC_Rosters_by_Era.md.

### 4. Live dangers

A traditional blood cult in the hedge-maze estate (Book 1, Ch9 L50); the Ustei raids (Book 1, Ch83 L4); Old City's "Big Three" crime lords (Book 1, Ch50 L92); star seeds implanted through a hidden cult (Book 2, Ch21 L26-28).

### 5. What the characters know

- **Jason is an outworlder**: the team and Danielle know by Book 1 (Book 1, Ch21 L82-87; Book 1, Ch48 L101-105).
- **Party interface**: Jason's allies use his party chat (Book 2, Ch50 L8; Book 2, Ch65 L89).
- **Star seeds and a cult's aim to seal an astral space**: known to Greenstone's elite from Book 2 (Book 2, Ch21 L19-28, L123).

### 6. Narrator language that stays spoiler-safe

- Say "a hidden cult" before Book 2, Ch21; say "star seeds" only after.
- Say "the goddess of Knowledge" without hinting at a plan.
- Do not mention the System as a world event, Earth's magic or the vampire war.

### 7. Leave closed

Who the Builder cult's leaders are beyond what has been shown; the Order of the Reaper's actual survival; Jason's return to Earth.

---

## Starter Pack: Era 2, Bronze and the Builder (Book 3)

### 1. Setting

- **Where and when**: Greenstone and the delta in a mild winter (Book 3, Ch1 L14), then the Builder's astral space from about month 7 until Jason's death at about month 15 to 16 (see ../../08_History_and_Timeline/Master_Timeline.md).
- **Scale**: Path of Shadows reaches bronze (Book 3, Ch29 L23); the astral space has twelve towers about eight kilometres apart (Book 3, Ch36 L36).
- **Turning point**: Jason dies covering the team's escape (Book 3, Ch69 L900).

### 2. Three starting situations

1. **Purity under inquiry**: an ecumenical council investigates the church of Purity (Book 3, Ch1 L105). *Who in the church knew?*
2. **A rain elemental over Old City**: it appears in the city, and bronze-rankers gear up to confront it (Book 3, Ch6 L18, L43). *Who takes the lead?*
3. **Hunting Killian Laurent**: Jason's team spends days looking for the man who possessed a star seed (Book 3, Ch24 L3). *Where did he run?*

### 3. Cast and voice cues

- **The team**: Jason, Humphrey, Sophie, Belinda, Clive, Neil (Book 3, Ch3 L70-82). Belinda is delighted by her own power (Book 2, Ch32 L72); Neil is tentative before he commits (Book 2, Ch27 L46).
- **Elders**: Danielle Geller, Thalia Mercer, Emir (voice cues in ../Tone_and_Voice.md).
- **Opponents**: Zato and Timos (Book 3, Ch7 L89; Book 2, Ch59 L87), Nicolas Hendren and Anisa Lasalle of Purity (Book 2, Ch19 L73; Book 2, Ch10 L92), Cole Silva and Killian Laurent (Book 3, Ch17 L14).
- **Cosmic visitors**: Dawn, Shako and Velius appear at the end (Book 3, Ch69 L233-238, L1036).

### 4. Live dangers

Star seeds; energy ghouls (Book 3, Ch62 L23); clockwork constructs and cores (Book 2, Ch25 L35-41); cult infiltrators among aristocrats; the astral space itself, called "a giant boarding vessel" (Book 3, Ch65 L79).

### 5. What the characters know

- **The cult exists and what it wants**: its aim is to reinforce an astral space and sever it from the world (Book 2, Ch21 L123).
- **The Rejector**: the cultists call Jason that (Book 3, Ch32 L105).

### 6. Narrator language that stays spoiler-safe

- Refer to "the cult's lord" or "the Builder" only as the characters do.
- Keep Dawn's identity as the World-Phoenix's servant in the background unless the scene is the Book 3 epilogue.
- Do not hint that Jason will go back to Earth.

### 7. Leave closed

The outcome of the three-way negotiation between the Builder, World-Phoenix and Reaper beyond what the scene shows (Book 3, Ch69 L279); why Jason's soul survived death.

---

## Starter Pack: Era 3, Earth (Books 4-6)

### 1. Setting

- **Where and when**: Australia, then Slovakia and France, from early June 2019 to a warm autumn day in 2021 (Book 4, Ch3 L112; Book 6, Ch47 L155).
- **Scale**: bronze at the start, silver by the Book 5 chapter "Silver" (Book 5, Ch31 L1).
- **The world**: the Engineers of Ascension hold Perth and Melbourne (Book 4, Ch9 L76); after the monster waves the Network is publicly the Global Defense Network (Book 5, Ch17 L7); transformation zones begin with Kostroma (Book 5, Ch44 L48-49).

### 2. Three starting situations

1. **The EOA moves on Sydney**: they have "seized control in Perth and Melbourne, and now they're making no secret of their overtures into Sydney" (Book 4, Ch9 L76). *Who in the family is in their path?*
2. **Reality cores**: one appears in each affected zone and the factions scramble for it (Book 5, Ch45 L9-11). *Who gets there first?*
3. **A domain nobody can enter safely**: gold-rankers explore Jason's Nitra domain until the hostile effects become dangerous (Book 6, Ch23 L3). *What do they want from it?*

### 3. Cast and voice cues

- **Jason**: dry understatement when grieving (Book 5, Ch20 L25).
- **Family**: Erika, Ian and Emi (Book 4, Ch3 L132), Hiro (Book 4, Ch1 L66-67), Ken and Cheryl (Book 4, Ch4 L105), Kaito and Amy (Book 5, Ch44 L3). Emi is sharp and political at thirteen (Book 5, Ch24 L3-5).
- **Network**: Anna Tilden (Book 5, Ch51 L48), Nigel Thornton (Book 4, Ch41 L91), Asya (Book 4, Ch36 L37); Mr North is amused and unhurried (Book 5, Ch41 L33).
- **Cabal**: Craig Vermillion (Book 4, Ch11 L101).
- **Pallimustus visitors**: Farrah is freed in Book 4 (Book 4, Ch51 L62); Dawn returns in a new avatar and is composed and dry (Book 5, Ch20 L21-24).

### 4. Live dangers

Proto-spaces opening in towns; ancient vampires fed on reality-core blood (Book 5, Ch55 L92); the EOA; the Network schism; Gerling (Book 6, Ch1 L3); Shako (Book 6, Ch17 L37).

### 5. What the characters know

- **Jason is an outworlder**: the Network learns it in Book 4 (Book 4, Ch18 L29; Book 4, Ch19 L69); the public learns of his personas (Book 5, Ch11 L12).
- **The magic link**: Dawn tells Jason of the siphoning and Purity's bargain (Book 4, Ch68 L59, L139).

### 6. Narrator language that stays spoiler-safe

- Call Jason "Mr Asano" to officials and "Jason" to family.
- Refer to "the other world" without the names Builder or Knowledge.
- Do not mention Jason's absence after Book 6 or the Earth vampire war's later phases.

### 7. Leave closed

The Network's founder; why the Cabal's leaders act as they do; the fate of the Earthlings after autumn 2021.

---

## Starter Pack: Era 4, Rimaros (Books 7-8)

### 1. Setting

- **Where and when**: the Storm Kingdom, a city of islands in the Sea of Storms (Book 7, Ch5 L56); Jason arrives in a cyclone as the surge begins (Book 7, Ch1 L56).
- **Scale**: silver rank in the Storm Kingdom, where sky islands house the cream of society (Book 7, Ch5 L65).
- **Turning point**: the Astral Throne and Gate appear (Book 8, Ch49 L105).

### 2. Three starting situations

1. **The outworlders**: a hundred and nineteen appear at once and must be placed (Book 7, Ch36 L86). *Who takes them in?*
2. **A political marriage that fell through**: Zara was matched with a member of House Irios for stability before the surge (Book 7, Ch18 L66-72). *Who benefits from the scandal?*
3. **A kidnapped heir**: Gibson Amouz is spirited away from the island (Book 8, Ch20 L4). *Where was he taken?*

### 3. Cast and voice cues

- **Royals**: Soramir (Book 7, Ch18 L42) is dignified; Zara is courtly with a threat in the smile (Book 2, Ch69 L17-19); Liara is brisk and official (Book 7, Ch17 L3).
- **Locals**: Pelli is blunt (Book 7, Ch13 L51); Argy is laconic (Book 7, Ch13 L31); Trenchant Moore is reserved (Book 7, Ch21 L13).
- **Team Biscuit**: named in Book 7 (Book 7, Ch56 L74); Gabrielle Pellin is stern and accusing of Jason's methods (Book 7, Ch9 L7).
- **Opponents**: Melody Jain and the Order of Redeeming Light (Book 8, Ch18 L3-6).

### 4. Live dangers

Invasion platforms (Book 7, Ch8 L36); the One Day War (Book 7, Ch66 L27); guild quotas that give guilds sixty percent of the surge quotas (Book 7, Ch12 L23); towns massacred under cover of monster attacks (Book 8, Ch9 L40).

### 5. What the characters know

- **Jason is an outworlder**: Soramir tells him he sensed his arrival (Book 7, Ch18 L42).
- **The Builder's invasion**: known to the royals and the Adventure Society (Book 7, Ch11 L5).

### 6. Narrator language that stays spoiler-safe

- Say "the Builder's forces" and "invasion platforms"; avoid hinting at who the Builder is.
- Call Dawn "the World-Phoenix's servant" only where a character does.
- Do not use Book 9 terms such as astral king, messenger war or world-taker worm.

### 7. Leave closed

Who else carried star seeds; the Order's inner structure beyond Melody and Sendira; the full outcome of the One Day War's casualty list.
